Requires Python 3.9 or newer and Vita3K; install instructions for Vita3K are included. ([mattsp1290/dotfiles](https://github.com/mattsp1290/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/vita3k-run-vpk))
- **vitable-health-webhooks**: Receive and verify Vitable Health webhooks. Use when setting up Vitable Health embedded-benefits webhook handlers, debugging X-Vitable-Signature (HMAC-SHA512) verification failures, or handling enrollment.accepted, enrollment.terminated, employee.eligibility_granted, and employee.deduction_created events. ([hookdeck/webhook-skills](https://github.com/hookdeck/webhook-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/vitable-health-webhooks) · ★102)
- **vitadeck-bench**: Measures VitaDeck performance with Benchmark Mode on the Vita, in Vita3K, or on the desktop host, and compares a run against the committed baseline. Use when the user asks about performance, CPU use, frame time, FPS, slow rendering or slow React updates, wants a benchmark or baseline, or wants to check whether a change made VitaDeck faster or slower. ([matemolnar8/vitadeck](https://github.com/matemolnar8/vitadeck/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/vitadeck-bench))
